- **interference**: Use when reasoning about interference — two wave amplitudes combine by phase, not classical sum: I = a1² + a2² + 2·a1·a2·cos(φ), constructive (in phase) reinforcing to (a1+a2)², destructive (out of phase) cancelling to (a1−a2)².
